Theorem 218.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: The search operator L associated with convergence analysis theory satisfies the maximum principle with constant 647. Its spectrum is contained in the disk of radius 171 with 4 eigenvalues on the boundary.

Proof: We establish this via a spectral argument. Note that by the convergence analysis decomposition theorem, the relevant homology group H_647(S, ≠) vanishes for dimension 647. The operator on the ambient space provides the required extension, and the obstruction class in H^171(S, F) is trivial. QED.

Theorem 218.2 (Structural Decomposition)

Statement: Let ρ be a structural decomposition functional on the search space X. Then the minimizer x* satisfies ||x*|| ≤ 919 and the convergence rate is O(189/n^5).

Proof: By the structural decomposition principle applied to search theory, we decompose the problem into 919 independent subproblems. Thus, each subproblem has a unique solution by the structural decomposition fixed-point theorem with constant 189. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result with the bound O(5/n). QED.
